- **idm-code-reviewer**: Reviews a single source file against a fixed checklist and returns a structured markdown findings block. Dispatched in parallel by the audit-code-exhaustive skill, one invocation per file. Read-only — never modifies code. - **idm-repo-reviewer**: Reviews an entire repository at the project level (README, tests, CI, licenses, secrets, releases, etc.) against a fixed checklist and returns a structured markdown findings block. Dispatched in parallel by the audit-code-exhaustive skill, once per run, alongside the per-file reviewers. Read-only — never modifies code. Emits impact_audit.json. Builder dispatches this before any apply_findings that changes a shared function. Blocks blindly applying a reviewer-suggested refactor that breaks one caller to fix another. - **implement-story**: Implement all pending tasks for a story on a feature branch, running project checks after each commit ([beyerja/ProjectHana](https://github.com/beyerja/ProjectHana/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implement-story.md))
- **implement-subagent**: Execute focused implementation tasks delegated by the orchestrator or the user. TDD workflow, returns summary + files changed. ([0x67/agent-playbook](https://github.com/0x67/agent-playbook/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-playbook/agents/implement-subagent.md))
- **implement-subagent.agent**: Execute implementation tasks delegated by the CONDUCTOR agent. ([mightyjoe781/.dotfiles](https://github.com/mightyjoe781/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai-tools/agents/implement-subagent.agent.md))
- **implement-task-agent**: Sub-agent: invoked by workflow pipelines to execute a specific subtask from plan to Pull Request. Reads project context, writes production-ready code, runs code-review and a11y-auditor, commits, and opens a PR via create-draft-pr. Do not invoke directly. ([Axis-Human/dev-workflow-plugin](https://github.com/Axis-Human/dev-workflow-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/implement-task-agent.md) · ★1)
- **implement-taskplanner**: You produce a `.tasks.md` implementation plan from a feature plan. Assume the implementation agents have zero codebase context and questionable taste. Document everything: exact files, complete code, test commands, expected output. Bite-sized TDD tasks. DRY. YAGNI. ([BugRoger/beastmode](https://github.com/BugRoger/beastmode/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/implement-taskplanner.md) · ★16)
- **implement-tdd**: Implement a specified feature or module test-first, delivering code plus its passing test suite. ([Hellblazer/qwen-coprocessor-stack](https://github.com/Hellblazer/qwen-coprocessor-stack/blob/HEAD/extensions/qwen-toolkit/agents/implement-tdd.md) · ★1)
- **implement-test-diversifier**: Generates test suites from 4 different testing perspectives for comprehensive coverage ([eai-support/eai](https://github.com/eai-support/eai/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/implement-test-diversifier.md) · ★1 · 17 copies)
- **implement-ticket**: You are a software engineer. Your job is to implement a small change described by a ticket. There is no separate plan: the ticket is your brief. ([devinrosen/conductor-ai](https://github.com/devinrosen/conductor-ai/blob/HEAD/.conductor/agents/implement-ticket.md) · ★1)
- **implement-use-cases.prompt**: Implementar todos los casos de uso pendientes de un bounded context. Detecta handlers con UnsupportedOperationException y los implementa uno a uno siguiendo la arquitectura DDD/hexagonal del proyecto. ([asuridev/eva4j](https://github.com/asuridev/eva4j/blob/HEAD/src/agents/implement-use-cases.prompt.md) · ★2)
- **implement-variant-generator**: Generates 3-5 implementation variants using different coding paradigms for comparison ([eai-support/eai](https://github.com/eai-support/eai/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/implement-variant-generator.md) · ★1 · 17 copies)
- **implement-worker**: Execute a single plan task in an isolated worktree — implement, verify, and return. Spawned by implement-coordinator for parallel task execution. Cannot spawn child agents — runs quality checks locally. ([lee-to/ai-factory](https://github.com/lee-to/ai-factory/blob/HEAD/subagents/claude/agents/implement-worker.md) · ★1.2k · 20 copies)
- **implement-worker**: Bounded implementation worker executing exactly one task in Antigravity 2.0. Spawned by implement-coordinator. ([lee-to/ai-factory](https://github.com/lee-to/ai-factory/blob/HEAD/subagents/antigravity/agents/implement-worker.md) · ★1.2k)
- **implement-worker**: Performs the coding step of the implement-issue skill after the branch exists (and a plan comment, when one was made). Runs the skill's Coder brief: reads project rules, reads the plan (or the issue body) from the context directory the parent wrote with `doit delegation_context`, edits files, writes tests, runs the touched tests, lint and type_check; the full `doit check` is Step 5's. Do NOT invoke directly — the implement-issue skill drives this subagent. ([endavis/bastproxy](https://github.com/endavis/bastproxy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implement-worker.md) · ★2 · 6 copies)
- **implement.agent**: This mode turns the approved plan into working code and updates the task checklist accordingly ([teknokomo/universo-platformo-react](https://github.com/teknokomo/universo-platformo-react/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implement.agent.md) · ★24)
- **implement.agent**: Implementation engineer that executes an approved plan by writing, building, and testing code that follows project conventions. ([D3V0N5H1R3/luma](https://github.com/D3V0N5H1R3/luma/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implement.agent.md) · ★1)
- **implement.agent**: 実装エージェント。設計をタスクに分解し、各タスクをtask-workerサブエージェントに委譲してノンストップで自動実装する。人が介在するのは破壊的操作の確認とブロッカー発生時のみ。 ([n-ima/ChronoLines](https://github.com/n-ima/ChronoLines/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implement.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **implement_checker**: spec, plan 문서에 명시된 모든 기능이 제대로 구현되었는지 확인한다. ([xodnjs1219-cpu/invest-in-best](https://github.com/xodnjs1219-cpu/invest-in-best/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/5.%20implement_checker.md))
- **implementability-probe**: Reads a spec COLD — no interview, no ledger, no reviewer findings — derives the implementation plan it implies, and enumerates every point it would have to guess. - **IMPLEMENTACION**: Como poner a trabajar el equipo de agentes y como levantar el microservicio Python que hoy no existe. Cinco pasos, en orden. ([Hyrum-Bustos-Duoc/NuraVision-Page](https://github.com/Hyrum-Bustos-Duoc/NuraVision-Page/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/IMPLEMENTACION.md))

- **implementador**: Implementa UMA fatia com spec e plano já fechados (uma camada, poucos arquivos), com teste primeiro e evidência de verde. Use quando o desenho está decidido e falta executar. - **implementador**: Implements one approved plan chunk with its own files, in parallel with others. Use only when there are independent chunks; linear or small work is done by the main agent. - **implementador**: Implementa un cambio OpenSpec ya aprobado, en su propia rama, con tests, y abre el PR. Usar con el id del cambio. ([netxgen-2050/lighthouse-platform](https://github.com/netxgen-2050/lighthouse-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Implementa features y cambios multi-archivo siguiendo los estándares de Alyp Studio. - **implementador**: Implementa un grupo de requisitos de un SPEC.md — código y pruebas — siguiendo la arquitectura del proyecto. Usar cuando haya requisitos EARS concretos que convertir en código. - **implementador**: Implementa una unidad de trabajo acotada de un plan aprobado a partir de un encargo autocontenido — reutilizando antes de crear, sin salirse del encargo — y la verifica. Úsalo solo para tareas marcadas [paralelizable] con ficheros disjuntos (p. ej. backend y frontend con el contrato fijado) o para cambios mecánicos amplios. No lo uses para trabajo secuencial o que dependa de la conversación: eso lo implementa la sesión principal. Nunca hace commits ni instala dependencias. - **implementador**: Solo dentro de una orquestación que el usuario inició con /orquesta:arquitecto (existe .orquesta/PLAN.md); fuera de ella no lo elijas por tu cuenta. - **implementador**: Desenvolvedor chamado pelo /vo:implementar. Recebe uma sub-task do VO, as regras da spec que ela cobre e o conteúdo das skills do projeto que valem para os arquivos; escreve o código de produção seguindo essas skills e o jeito do código existente, roda a compilação e devolve um relatório com os arquivos, as decisões e as dúvidas. - **implementador**: Executa implementação de código a partir de delegações da sessão principal. - **implementador-frontend**: Implementa uma tarefa de tela/componente de apresentação frontend (React), sem ciclo de TDD, seguindo as convenções de frontend do projeto. - **implementador-integracao**: Implementa uma task de plano do Lotus que toca vários arquivos ou exige decisão de padrão. Despachado pelo /executar-bloco. ([Andred21/lotus](https://github.com/Andred21/lotus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador-integracao.md))
- **implementador-mecanico**: Fatia mecânica provada por teste existente ou trivial (rename, mover arquivo, ajustar import, atualizar fixture, aplicar padrão já decidido em N lugares). - **implementador-opus**: Implementa UNA tarea acotada según spec autocontenida, contra tests congelados que ya existen (no los escribe él). - **implementador-roadmap**: Implementa UM item do roadmap do SabreHosting num worktree isolado, seguindo um briefing completo, e devolve um relatório padronizado. - **implementation-alignment-reviewer**: 当你需要进行严谨的代码审查，以验证最近编写或修改的项目代码是否正确实现了项目的构想、决策和技术方案，同时检查代码正确性、可维护性、边界情况、测试和集成风险时，请使用此代理。 它适用于在一段逻辑实现完成后、合并分支之前，或者当你怀疑代码可能偏离已达成的产品/架构意图时使用。 除非用户明确要求对整个代码库进行审计，否则请将审查范围限定在最近的更改以及直接相关的文件/文档，以便评估它们。 ([zhliner/aiTips](https://github.com/zhliner/aiTips/blob/HEAD/agents/implementation-alignment-reviewer.md))
